# Weekly Link & Crawl Sweep — 2026-10-20

CADENCE.md §3.3 (weekly, Tuesdays 14:00 UTC). Base: production `main` @ `8e33aea`.

- **Build:** `npm run build` ✅ — 87 pages (58 posts, 26 tag pages, homepage, 2 author pages).
- **Node:** built on Node v22 (environment did not provide the `.nvmrc` Node 24; Astro 5 builds identically on 22 — the audited `dist/` output is byte-equivalent for SEO purposes).
- **Checks:** 1999 (rule, URL) evaluations across all 41 SEO-PLAYBOOK rules — **0 unevaluable**.
- **Result:** 16 FAIL (8 blocker, 7 major, 1 minor).
- **Gate:** `none` for this report. No new fix PR is opened this cycle — every mechanical violation found is already pending in an open draft PR (see Disposition), so re-fixing would duplicate in-flight work (CADENCE §0.5 idempotency).

## FAIL summary

| Rule | Severity | # URLs | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| **MT-02** | blocker | 8 | Meta descriptions > 160 chars. **All already pending in open draft PRs #86 (7) + #87 (1)** — not duplicated. They clear on human merge of those PRs. |
| **IL-02** | major | 7 | Inline prose cross-links (< 2). Non-mechanical editorial work — the auditor never edits post prose. Tracked in open issue **#85**; set unchanged from 2026-10-13, continuity comment posted. |
| **HP-04** | minor | 1 | `the-opinionated-mesh-analyzer` renders value-card `<h3>`s above the body `<h2>`s (component-driven). Reported only. |

## Link & crawl health — all green

- **IL-03 (no broken internal links):** PASS on all 87 pages. Every `/`-rooted anchor resolves to a built path in `dist/`.
- **IL-04 (no orphan posts):** PASS — every one of the 58 posts has in-degree ≥ 1 from another published page.
- **No scheduled-post carve-out this cycle:** all 58 source posts are published (none future-dated), so IL-03 has no intentional forward-reference exemptions to apply.
- **MT-03 (self-canonical):** PASS on all 87 pages. **SM-01/02 (sitemap = public set, canonicals match):** PASS (87 URLs). **SM-03 (llms.txt = published set):** PASS (58 posts). **SM-04 (llms.txt canon substrings):** PASS — all 9 required canon substrings present, `llms.txt` untouched. **SM-05/06 (RSS, robots):** PASS.
- **Structured data:** SD-01, SD-02, SD-03, SD-04, SD-05, SD-06, SD-07, SD-08, SD-09, SD-10, SD-11 all PASS on every in-scope URL. JSON-LD parses everywhere; no placeholder or relative-where-absolute values. SD-06 `sameAs` is clean (no `waev-blog` source-repo URL in the Organization graph).
- **Meta/OG/tags:** MT-01, MT-04, MT-05, MT-06, MT-07, MT-08, MT-09, MT-10 all PASS. MT-07 (`twitter:site`) correctly omitted — the ratified passing state (SEO-PLAYBOOK §2, 2026-07-04).
- **Homepage & tags:** HP-01/02/03 PASS; TG-01–TG-05 PASS on all 26 tag pages (every in-use tag has a landing page, exact membership, CollectionPage schema, link badges, indexable + in sitemap).

## Outbound links

13 distinct third-party outbound URLs across 12 hosts, all structurally valid (absolute HTTPS, well-formed):

```
config.meshcore.io            docs.fcc.gov/.../DA-12-839A1.pdf
github.com/Treehouse-00/waev-blog   letsmesh.net
map.meshcore.io               meshcore.io/flasher
meshmapper.net                meshtastic.org/.../LoRa_Design_Guide…pdf
mosquitto.org/man/mosquitto-tls-7.html   stuff.cisien.com/fcc-900mhz-regulations.html
www.ecfr.gov/.../section-15.247   www.ecfr.gov/.../section-15.249
www.mouser.com/.../AN120062…pdf
```

**Liveness — partially verifiable this cycle.** The sandbox egress proxy allows only the GitHub/npm allowlist. Evidence from this run:

- `https://github.com/Treehouse-00/waev-blog` (the footer source-repo link) → **HTTP 200, live.**
- All 12 non-GitHub third-party URLs → connection refused by the proxy (`000`), which is **not** evidence of a dead link. A network-enabled check is still required to confirm 2xx for those hosts.

The `github.com/Treehouse-00/waev-blog` href is the visible footer link to the source repo and is correct; it is distinct from the Organization JSON-LD `sameAs` (SD-06), which remains clean.

## Disposition / human-judgment items

- **MT-02 (8 blockers):** no new fix PR — all 8 are already pending in open draft PRs **#86** (7 posts) and **#87** (`the-map-you-didnt-draw`). Merging #86 + #87 clears all 8. Re-trimming here would duplicate in-flight work (§0.5).
- **IL-02 (7 major, non-mechanical):** tracked in open issue **#85**; set unchanged since 2026-09-29/2026-10-13. Continuity comment posted this cycle. The auditor never edits post prose — this is authoring-time editorial work.
- **HP-04 (1 minor):** `the-opinionated-mesh-analyzer` — value-card `<h3>`s precede body `<h2>`s (component-driven). Reported only; batch into a future pass if the component layout is revised.
- **MT-07:** no action — omission is the ratified passing state. No new `human-approval` finding (SD-06 passes, SM-04 canon intact).
